# Atlas Capital — Progress Tracker

Update this file at the start and end of **every** stage (see `02-stage-protocol.md §3, §6`).

> **Rules (from [`Project.md`](../Project.md)):** statuses are `NOT STARTED` / `IN PROGRESS` / `BLOCKED` / `DONE`. A box may be ticked, and a stage set to `DONE`, **only after** a *Verification evidence* block with real output from that stage's chat is written in the stage folder's `verification.md` and summarised below (Rule 1). Each stage's records live in its folder `stages/stage-NN-<slug>/` (Rule 2A). Stage N cannot start until Stage N−1 is `DONE` (Rule 4).

The CLI header says "72 dates" while the ICs cover 71 (`summary["ic"][…]["n_dates"]`); the header could show dates with an IC, or the window could start after the warm-up
- (Stage 18) No `DataView` convenience: consumers (Stages 19, 20, 42, 43) call `view.returns(symbols, lookback)` and then `covariance_estimator(method).estimate(…)` / `forecast_vols(…)`; add a `covariance_from_view` helper (with a lookback in config) when the first consumer needs one
- (Stage 18) GARCH gives a one-step (next-session) forecast only; multi-day horizons (mean reversion to the long-run variance, reported in `params`) are not exposed — add when Stage 20 (multi-day VaR) or Stage 43 (sizing horizon) needs them. (one fit costs ~0.007 s on 1,000 sessions, measured: ~4 s for 500 names)
- (Stage 18) Ledoit-Wolf/OAS under `pairwise` average each moment over the pair's common sessions and use the mean pair count as `T` (exact for complete data, an approximation otherwise; on the real lake 1.25% of cells are missing)
- (Stage 18) `missing="complete"` is all-or-nothing: on the real lake (2021, S&P 500) a few late-listed names leave 60 common sessions < 63 → `DataError`. A policy that drops the names limiting the common window could be added if a consumer needs complete-case estimates (pairwise is the default)
- (Stage 18) Sample and EWMA covariances with more names than sessions are singular and are always eigenvalue-floored at `max_condition` (1e5); consumers that invert a covariance (Stages 41–42) should use Ledoit-Wolf/OAS (`default_method` ledoit_wolf)
- (Stage 18 audit F) `VolForecast` is a frozen dataclass with a `params` mapping, so it compares by value but is unhashable; make it hashable (e.g. a tuple of items) if a consumer needs forecasts in sets or as dict keys
- (Stage 19) `factor_panel` (Stage 16, via `DataView.fundamentals_panel` / `fundamentals_history`, per-symbol loops) costs ~136 s per as-of on ~500 names (cProfile, real lake): a real-data risk-model build is dominated by it (2018–2024 with monthly anchors ≈ 3 h; the evidence run used `anchor_months: 3`), and `style_inputs` for one as-of is far above the brief's 2 s (the model build itself from a fitted history is ~0.02 s). Vectorise the fundamentals reads, or let `style_inputs` / the loader read the `FactorStore` cache (it holds `raw.size` = ln cap, so caps are recoverable) once the real lake's cache is filled
- (Stage 19) Daily returns are not truncated before the regression (Barra caps extreme returns): a bad print that passes the data-quality checks moves that day's factor returns; add a robust cap (e.g. |r| ≤ k·cross-sectional MAD) if real-data residuals show it
- (Stage 19) Fundamental styles and the regression universe change only at anchors (monthly by default): a name joining the S&P 500 mid-month enters at the next anchor, a leaver stays until it; caps roll with the adjusted close (includes dividends, a small upward drift in caps between anchors)
- (Stage 19) Names blocked by today's data-quality checks are left out of the whole loaded window (the price panel is read from one view at the window end)
- (Stage 19, data coverage — Stages 07/08/09) On the real lake the risk model covers ~387 of ~503 point-in-time S&P 500 members per day (2018–2024 mean). Measured on 2018-03-01 / 2023-03-01: members without a bar that day 74 / 5 (former members' price history missing), members without a GICS sector in `instruments` 51 / 53 (the table has 387 rows without a sector), members without a market cap from `factor_panel` (no/stale share count) 36 / 34. The model drops them by design (never a guessed sector or cap); backfill sectors for historical members (Stage 08 reference data), prices of delisted members (Stage 07) and share counts (Stage 09) to raise coverage
- (Stage 19) Done 2026-11-11 (Phase 2 checkpoint, Tier 1): `atlas risk-model build --from --to [--store] [--etfs/--no-etfs]`; real-lake trial 271 sessions, 10 snapshots, 30 ETFs. The main lake's `factor_returns` / `risk_model_snapshots` are built (2026-11-12, Tier 1 item 4: 2,471 sessions from 2017-01-03, 115 snapshots from 2017-04-28); the daily append belongs to Stage 55 / 61 (was: No CLI for `build_and_store` (the brief lists none): Stage 61 (scheduler) / 65 (CLI) should wire a daily `factor_returns` append and month-end snapshots; writing to the main lake needs the owner's terminal from this sandbox)
- (Stage 19) The ETF betas of the constraint direction are not identified (only the part orthogonal to Σ share_s f_s = 0 is); the ridge returns the minimum-norm one, which leaves every risk number unchanged but makes individual sector betas of broad ETFs depend on that choice
- (Stage 20) Monte Carlo VaR draws multivariate-normal scenarios from a covariance, so for a linear book it converges to the normal VaR; fat-tailed scenarios (Student-t, filtered historical bootstrap, factor-model simulation) belong with Stage 46 stress testing
- (Stage 20) Multi-day VaR uses √h scaling or overlapping h-session returns; a GARCH term structure (Stage 18 Backlog: one-step GARCH only) would give a conditional multi-day VaR
- (Stage 20) The default risk-free rate is a config scalar (`quant.metrics.risk_free_annual`, 0); every function also accepts a per-session Series, so the tear sheet (Stage 57) can pass a FRED T-bill series (DGS3MO / DTB3, Stage 10) when it needs excess returns
- (Stage 20) Cornish-Fisher VaR is refused outside its domain since the 2026-11-05 review, and daily equity returns are usually outside it (SPY 2020–2024: S −0.54, K 11.5). If a parametric fat-tail VaR is wanted (Stage 44 / 63), add Maillard's corrected Cornish-Fisher (re-fitted parameters inside the domain) or a Student-t / filtered-historical VaR; `historical` remains the limits' method
- (Stage 20) Historical-simulation portfolio VaR drops sessions where a held name has no return; a book holding a recent IPO gets a short sample (`DataError` below `var_min_obs`); a proxy-fill rule (sector ETF returns) could keep the window long — decide in Stage 44

- (Stage 21 → **Stage 43**, owner decision 2026-11-05) **Walk-forward comparison of four risk-scalar rules; adopt the out-of-sample winner after costs.** Until then `quant.regime.risk_scalar` (today's table) stays the default. In Stage 43 (sizing, stops & hedging; it builds on Stage 42's vol targeting `target_vol × regime.risk_scalar`), on a walk-forward basis (rules and any learned parameters fitted only on data before each test window; out of sample from `backtest.in_sample_end`, spec A7), compare the fund scaled by: (1) **fully invested** (scalar 1.0 always); (2) **the current table** (vol × trend × stress × HMM × quadrant); (3) **the table without the quadrant** (quadrant multipliers 1.0); (4) **a learned model** mapping the `RegimeState` readings to the scalar (fitted walk-forward, same [0.25, 1] bounds). Score each out of sample **after costs** (Stage 51 cost model: turnover from rescaling × half-spread and impact) on the same target vol; adopt the winner as the default `risk_scalar` rule and record the comparison and the decision. Context: on the real lake the current table cut risk on 35.9% of calm 2017 days purely through the quadrant (Stage 21 verification)
- (Stage 21) The 3-state HMM's turbulent state has P ≥ 0.5 on 62% of 2018 days (the top state absorbs moderately elevated vol after the calm 2015–2017 training window): calibrate in Stage 58 (2 states, a higher `turbulent_prob`, or a longer `train_start` once the lake holds pre-2015 prices)
- (Stage 21) Done 2026-11-11 for the block list (Phase 2 checkpoint, Tier 1, Stage 13 code): the cost was not the macro vintages but `check_fundamentals_jumps` (97% of 71–97 s profiled), a warning-only check; `DataView.blocked` now runs only the checks that can block (0.8–1.2 s on the main lake, block lists equal on six days). Still open: the full report (`quality_issues()`, `atlas data quality`, the sync's `quality` step) is as slow as before — see the Phase 2 checkpoint items below (was: Stage 13's quality report costs ~51 s per new as-of on the real lake (it scans every macro vintage and the recent bars); any per-day `DataView` loop pays it (Stage 17 measured ~16 s per view). Optimise (filter macro vintages by the window, cache across consecutive days) at the next phase checkpoint)
- (Stage 21) `regime_history` uses the reading view's quality block list (a symbol blocked only on an earlier day is kept for that day; real lake: EOG, HP on 2020-03-16). A per-day block list needs persisted `data_quality_issues` (`quality_report(..., persist=True)` by the daily cycle, Stage 50s) or a faster report
- (Stage 21) ~~No CLI builds or persists the regime history~~ — fixed 2026-11-05 (gap fix): `atlas regime build --from --to [--store]`; the main lake's `regime_history` holds 2016-01-04 .. 2026-11-02. Keeping it current daily belongs to the daily cycle (Stage 50s)
- (Stage 21) HY OAS: the stress z-score switches from the proxy to the OAS level once 252 OAS observations are known (from about 2024-11); the HMM always uses the proxy. Revisit if a longer credit series is added (FRED `BAA10Y`, a licensed OAS feed)

- (Stage 22, brief Out of scope) ML alpha: gradient boosting on the Stage 15/16 features with purged, embargoed CV
- (Stage 22) TSMOM `sharpe_prior` (0.3) and PEAD `drift_ann` (0.06) are literature priors; their in-sample calibration slopes were negative (TSMOM −1.54, dominated by the ETFs' average return level; PEAD −0.34). Re-estimate (or shrink to 0) walk-forward once a calibration routine runs over the backtest (Stage 53 prediction scoring)
- (Stage 22) ~106 S&P 500 members per date have no bars in the lake (former members without a Yahoo history, Stage 14) and get no alpha; the in-sample cross-section is the ~430 names with prices. Fill the price history (another vendor) before trusting survivorship-free alpha statistics
- (Stage 22 → Stage 58) **Out-of-sample check of the multi-factor composite** (walk-forward and the 2022+ hold-out) before any book relies on it. The multi-factor weights (quality 0.62, reversal 0.38, others 0) are an **in-sample fit** on 2016–2021 (owner decision 2026-11-07); two composite specifications were tried (equal weights, IC −0.006; fitted, IC +0.026). Count both as trials for the deflated Sharpe / PBO, and judge the composite only on walk-forward and the 2022+ hold-out; the point-in-time IC weighting (`weighting: ic`) was negative in-sample (−0.017)
- (Stage 22) Multi-factor realised calibration slope is +0.07 (prior IC 0.03 vs realised Pearson IC 0.017, fat tails): `exp_return_ann` over-states the size; re-estimate the IC walk-forward (Stage 53 prediction scoring / Stage 58) before sizing on it
- (Stage 22) Done 2026-11-12 (Phase 2 checkpoint, Tier 2): `predict` raises `ConfigError` when the stated horizon differs from the config it uses (all three models). Was: `MultiFactorModel.horizon` reports `config.horizon` (or the code default); a model built without `config` follows the view's `quant.alpha.multifactor` in `predict`, so read the horizon from the same config when they can differ

- (Stage 23, brief Out of scope) Baskets / Johansen cointegration; Kalman-filtered rolling hedge ratio (the brief's alternative to OLS)
- (Stage 23) The pair filters' false-positive rate is the Engle-Granger test's own size (~4% of prefiltered same-sector pairs; half-life and Hurst barely bind on random walks): with hundreds of candidates per formation many selected pairs can be spurious. Add multiple-testing control (e.g. Benjamini-Hochberg over a formation's candidates) or out-of-sample pair validation (a second window) and compare walk-forward in Stage 58
- (Stage 23) Weights are dollar-neutral per pair (brief), not β-neutral: with β ≠ 1 the position is not the traded spread `log y − β log x`; compare β-weighted legs in Stage 39 / 58
- (Stage 23) No borrow or financing cost in the pairs backtest (the short legs); the cost model is 5 bps per unit turnover. Stage 51 transaction-cost models should replace it
- (Stage 23) Sectors come from today's instruments table (static), as in Stage 22: a name that changed GICS sector is paired by its current one
- (Stage 23 → Stage 39 / 58) **In-sample pairs edge is thin after costs**: 2017–2021 gross Sharpe 0.83, net 0.10 (5 bps per unit turnover, ~300 round trips a year; mean trade +0.14% per unit pair gross vs 0.10% round-trip cost); 19% of exits are forced by monthly deselection. Candidates to test walk-forward (not to tune on 2017–2021): cost-aware entry (expected reversion > round-trip cost), keeping open pairs through reselection unless broken, β-neutral legs
- (Phase 2 checkpoint) Done 2026-11-12: Tier 1 item 4. `factor_exposures` (142 month-ends from 2015-01-30) and `factor_returns` / `risk_model_snapshots` (2,471 sessions from 2017-01-03, 115 snapshots from 2017-04-28) are on the main lake and read back ([`READ-BACK OK`](stages/stage-23-stat-arb/artifacts/phase2-checkpoint-2026-11-11-main-lake-readback.txt)); bias statistic out of sample: cap-weighted 1.094, equal-weighted 1.076, random long/short 1.105, style tilts 0.960–1.067, single names 1.067
- (Phase 2 checkpoint → Stage 13) The `stale_price` rule blocks `BIL` (a T-bill ETF) on days when its close is unchanged for 5–7 sessions with volume (seen at 2020-11-30, 2021-10-29, 2022-03-31): a false alarm for a cash-like instrument near zero rates. Those three month-end risk-model snapshots therefore carry 29 ETFs instead of 30. Decide: exempt cash-like ETFs, or a tick-size-aware rule
- (Phase 2 checkpoint → Stage 19) An ETF the view withholds (on the block list) is absent from the snapshot without a log line: `etf_exposures` logs `risk_model.etf_dropped` only for names it receives. Log the requested ETFs that the view did not serve
- (Phase 2 checkpoint → Stage 19) `build_and_store` spends about 103 s per month-end snapshot on the real lake (3 h 22 min of the 4.7 h ten-year build): `build_model_from_view` → `style_inputs` builds the exposure inputs afresh (`factor_panel`, 38 s per anchor in the same run, plus `compute_features`) although `load_cross_sections` has just read the same months as anchors and `factor_exposures` now stores them. Reuse the anchors or the stored cache; not profiled further
- (Phase 2 checkpoint → Stage 19 / 42) The stored models under-predict risk by 6–10% for diversified portfolios out of sample (bias 1.08–1.10 over 114 months, 2017–2026), where the 2018–2024 evidence run had 1.05–1.14. Look at it when the optimiser's risk target is set (volatility-regime adjustment is the usual remedy)
- (Phase 2 checkpoint) Tier 2 items 1–6 done 2026-11-12. **Tier 2 item 7 waits for the owner's decision** (net debt / EBITDA with D&A: add the D&A tags to `config/xbrl_map.yaml`, re-run the fundamentals ingest from the raw filings kept in the lake, switch `net_debt_ebitda` to EBIT + D&A; it changes the factor settings hash, so `factor_exposures` (4.2 h) and the risk-model stores (4.7 h) on the main lake must be rebuilt). Tiers 3–4 of the [2026-11-11 report](stages/stage-23-stat-arb/artifacts/phase2-checkpoint-2026-11-11-report.md), each waiting for the owner's word: sectors for the 387 instruments without one, market caps, cheaper `resolve_concept` / `quarterly_values`, consumers reading the caches, suite time (Tier 3); lazy CLI imports, a pairs backtest command and research options, `covariance_from_view`, the layout test and dot-folders, share-volume features (Tier 4)
- (Phase 2 checkpoint → Stages 53 / 58) The hold-out guard is in `atlas research signal` only: `research_signal`, `forward_returns`, `pairs_backtest` and stage artifact scripts are not guarded (they are run out of sample on purpose later). A script that reads past `backtest.in_sample_end` should call `holdout_check` and say so; the pairs backtest command (Tier 4) must use it
- (Phase 2 checkpoint → Stage 17) The headline IC t-statistic still squeezes dates without an IC out before the Newey-West sum; only the by-regime table keeps the gaps. They agree unless an IC is missing between two others; switch the headline to `keep_gaps=True` if that ever happens on real data
- (Phase 2 checkpoint → Stage 17) No mutation run was made for the Tier 2 changes (`holdout.py`, `keep_gaps`, `BacktestError`, `check_horizon`)
- (Phase 2 checkpoint) The unit suite takes 6 min 46 s on an idle machine (protocol §5 says under 60 s until Phase 8; CI 15–20 min); 378 s of it are the Phase 2 packages (statarb 122 s, factors 98 s). Tier 3 item 5
- (Phase 2 checkpoint) Done 2026-11-12 (Phase 2 checkpoint, Tier 2): `atlas research signal` refuses a window whose labels close after it unless `--allow-holdout` (audited). Was: `backtest.in_sample_end` (spec A7) is read by nothing outside config validation: `atlas research signal --to 2024` runs on the hold-out without a word. Tier 2 item 1
- (Phase 2 checkpoint) After the block-list fix a real-lake `factor_panel` still costs 33 s – 4 min per month-end (Stage 09 `resolve_concept` / `quarterly_values` per symbol and concept), so a full factor-cache build is ~3 h and a risk-model build ~4 h. Tier 3 items 3 and 4
- (Stage 24) Claude refusals: `AnthropicProvider` raises `LLMError` on `stop_reason: "refusal"`. The API's server-side `fallbacks` (beta) would re-run the request on another model, which then needs its own price row and ledger model; decide if the optional provider is ever used for real.
- (Stage 24) `claude-haiku-5-5` costs 0.50 / 2.50 USD per 1M tokens for prompts above 100K tokens; the price table has one rate per model (0.10 / 0.50), so such a call would be under-costed. Add a prompt-size tier to `ModelPrice` before using Haiku with very long prompts.
- (Stage 24 → 26) Tool loops: the gateway passes `ToolSpec`s and returns `ToolCall`s, but does not replay provider reasoning between tool rounds (DeepSeek `reasoning_content`, Claude thinking blocks). Stage 26's loop must check what each provider needs; `LLMResult` may need an opaque provider-state field.
- (Stage 24 → 25) The cache key needs the schema, tools, temperature and `thinking` mode of `ProviderRequest`; the gateway adds `DATA_POLICY` to the system text before the provider sees it (hash the request after that step). A cache hit should write an `llm_usage` row with `cache_hit = true` and cost 0 (the column exists).
- (Stage 24) DeepSeek prices and peak hours are a snapshot of 2026-11-09 in `config/fund.yaml`; Chinese public holidays (off-peak at DeepSeek) are priced as peak. Re-check prices before the first paper-trading run (Stage 60) and when a model id changes.
- (Stage 24) No streaming and no async client: a deep call blocks up to `llm.request_timeout_seconds` (120 s). Stage 28's parallel fan-out can use threads (the gateway, ledger and budget guard are thread-safe).
- (Stage 24) `per_run_usd` (25) and `per_agent_call_max_tokens` (8000, reasoning tokens included) are still provisional; revisit with real agent prompts (Stage 29) and the first LLM backtest (Stage 56).
- (Stage 24) The unit suite takes 6.5 min locally and 15+ min in CI (4,037 tests), far above the stage protocol's "under 60 s until Phase 8" (§5). Profile the slowest tests (`pytest --durations=30`) and consider `pytest-xdist` in CI; the CI job limit is 40 minutes since 2026-11-10.

- Next stage should know: `src/atlas/core/errors.py` already exists and contains exactly two classes: `AtlasError(Exception)` ("Base class for every error raised by Atlas code") and `ConfigError(AtlasError)`; its module docstring says Stage 03 extends it. `atlas.core.config` imports `ConfigError` from it, and the only test touching it is `test_config_error_is_atlas_error` in `tests/unit/core/test_config.py`; there is no `tests/unit/core/test_errors.py` yet. Stage 03 adds `LookAheadError`, `DataError`, `LLMError`, `RiskLimitError`, `ComplianceError` and the hierarchy tests, and must keep both existing classes and `ConfigError` subclassing `AtlasError` (owner-confirmed 2026-10-26; Stage 03's brief is unchanged). `config_fingerprint` is ready for `RunContext`; take `mode` from `cfg.profile`. Every config failure raises `ConfigError` whose message names the file, variable or dotted key and never a value; `ValidationError`s are re-raised `from None` so pasted secrets can't reach a traceback. The pod files define the pod set (deleting one removes the pod; each file is a full definition); to switch a pod off, set `enabled: false` (its budget stays unused, budgets must still sum to 1.0). Unit tests must keep the autouse `_isolated_env` pattern (clears `ATLAS__*` and the secret vars, `chdir(tmp_path)`) and must not load `repo_root/"config"` directly, because `load_config` reads the `.env` next to `config/`. In this sandbox, a new worktree needs `UV_PROJECT_ENVIRONMENT=.venv.nosync uv sync --group dev` + `ln -s .venv.nosync .venv` and `UV_CACHE_DIR="$TMPDIR/uv-cache"`

Runtime deps structlog 26.1.0, python-ulid 4.0.1
- Key interfaces: `with run_context(mode="backtest", as_of=date(2024, 1, 2)) as ctx:` or `run_context(config=cfg, as_of=...)` (mode = `cfg.profile`, fingerprint = `config_fingerprint(cfg)`); nested `run_context(as_of=..., stage=...)` keeps `run_id`/`mode`/fingerprint; `current_run_context() -> RunContext | None`; `configure_logging(*, level="INFO", log_file=None, console=None)`; `get_logger(__name__)`; `AuditLog(path).record(EventType.X, actor, payload) -> AuditEvent`; `AuditLog.verify_chain(path) -> bool`; `audit_path(cfg.data.lake_path, ctx.run_id)`; `canonical_json(obj) -> str`
- Decisions: Decisions log rows dated 2026-10-26, stage 03 (D1–D11)
- Collisions with the overall plan: none with the roadmap or spec; one tension inside the conventions (wall-clock rule vs `AuditEvent.ts`), resolved by D2 without Stage 05 code
- Process deviations (records only, found by the post-DONE audit): see the Decisions log row "Stage 03 has two commits"
- Deviations from brief: none in scope. Additions: `current_run_context()`, `run_dir()`, `audit_path()`, `reset_logging()`, `GENESIS_HASH`; `run_context(config=...)` as well as `mode=`; `AuditLog` refuses to open a file whose chain is broken
- Known gaps → backlog: no external anchor for the chain; one writer process per audit file (see Backlog, "Stage 03")
- Next stage should know: Stage 04's `new_id(prefix)` should build on python-ulid (already a dependency); Stage 03's `run_id`/`event_id` are bare 26-char ULIDs (no prefix). `RunContext` and `AuditEvent` are frozen Pydantic models with `extra="forbid"`. `record()` raises `AtlasError` outside a run context, and normalises the payload with `canonical_json` first (`Decimal` → string, aware datetimes → UTC ISO; NaN/inf, naive datetimes and sets raise). Event kinds are a `StrEnum`; later stages add members (Stage 26 `AGENT_REPORT`, Stage 60 `RECON_BREAK`). A new `threading.Thread` has no run context: start it with `contextvars.copy_context().run`. Logging tests must call `reset_logging()` in teardown (handlers sit on the root logger). There is still no `atlas.core.clock`: the only time sources are python-ulid and structlog internals, and the grep for `datetime.now|date.today|time.time|utcnow` over `src/` and `tests/` is empty, ready for Stage 05's banned-API rule. Stage 06 mirrors `AuditLog` into DuckDB: read the JSONL lines, they are the source of truth. In this sandbox, a new worktree needs `UV_PROJECT_ENVIRONMENT=.venv.nosync uv sync --group dev` + `ln -s .venv.nosync .venv` and `UV_CACHE_DIR="$TMPDIR/uv-cache"`

No new dependencies, no config keys
- Key interfaces: `from atlas.core.types import X` (everything is in `__all__`); `new_id(IdPrefix.THESIS) -> "th_<ULID>"`; `TargetPortfolio.book()/gross()/net()/long_leg()/short_leg()/diff(other)` act on `weights + hedges`; `OrderStatus.is_terminal`; `Order.remaining_quantity`/`signed_quantity`; `Fill.notional`; `Position.market_value(price)`; `NAVRecord.gross_exposure()/net_exposure()`; `Prediction.is_settled`; `ComplianceResult.passed`; `Direction.sign`, `Side.sign`
- Decisions: Decisions log rows dated 2026-10-26, stage 04 (C1, C2, D1–D9, review fix pass)
- Collisions with the overall plan: C1 (`LedgerEntry` in spec §7 but not the brief → created, spec wins); C2 (brief's "IDs are ULIDs" vs Stages 11/26's source-derived evidence ids → ULIDs only for minted ids)
- Deviations from brief: none in scope. Additions: `PodProposal.id` (spec §3 principle 5), `LedgerEntry` (C1), helper types (`Real`, `HorizonDays`, `AssetView`, `Claim`, `Catalyst`, `Breach`, `Violation`, `Forecast`, `EntrySide`, `DebateRole`, `RiskStatus`, `Severity`), `AtlasModel.model_copy` re-validation (review fix)
- Known gaps → backlog: review minors and the D8 deferred fields (see Backlog, "Stage 04")
- Next stage should know: every contract is a frozen `AtlasModel` (`extra="forbid"`, NaN/inf rejected); change one with `model_copy(update={...})`, which re-validates (never mutate). Sequences are tuples; mapping fields are `FrozenDict`s (read-only, hashable, dump as dicts). Symbols are normalised to `BRK.B` on input everywhere; use `vendor_symbol(s, "-")` for yfinance. Money, share quantities and trade prices are `Decimal`; weights/returns/confidence are `float` (bool rejected on LLM-facing fields). Timestamps (`UtcDatetime`) must be tz-aware and are stored in UTC; trading days are `date`. Ids are required on reload (no default factory): mint with `new_id(IdPrefix.X)`. `Evidence.id` is a source key such as `news:<id>`, not a ULID. `View.exp_return` is annualised while `InvestmentThesis.exp_return`/`Forecast.returns` are over the horizon (Backlog). There is still no `atlas.core.clock`: Stage 05 creates it; `Order.created_at`, `Fill.ts`, `Prediction.made_at`, `Lesson.created_at` are passed in by callers, and the wall-clock grep over `src/` and `tests/` is empty. Tests in `tests/unit/` have no `__init__.py`, so new test basenames must be unique (this stage uses `test_types_*.py`). In this sandbox, a new worktree needs `UV_PROJECT_ENVIRONMENT=.venv.nosync uv sync --group dev` + `ln -s .venv.nosync .venv` and `UV_CACHE_DIR="$TMPDIR/uv-cache"` (and PyPI reachable for the sync)

- Folder: [stages/stage-05-clock-pit/](stages/stage-05-clock-pit/) (`plan.md`, `worklog.md`, `verification.md`, `artifacts/`)

Runtime deps exchange-calendars 4.13.2, pandas 3.1.0 (numpy 2.5.4); dev dep pandas-stubs
- Key interfaces: `cal = TradingCalendar(config=cfg.calendar)`; `cal.is_session(d)`, `cal.sessions(a, b)` (inclusive tuple), `cal.next_session(d)`/`prev_session(d)` (strict), `cal.offset(d, n)`, `cal.session_open(d)`/`session_close(d)` (UTC), `cal.is_early_close(d)`; `with use_clock(SimClock(start=date(2020, 1, 2), calendar=cal)) as clock: get_clock().now()`, `clock.advance()`, `clock.set(ts)`; `AsOf.for_session(day, config=cfg.calendar)` → `.day`, `.cutoff` (UTC), `.knows(ts)`; `bar_available_at(day, cal)`; `to_available_at(ts)`; `pit_filter(df, as_of, time_col="available_at")`; `assert_no_lookahead(df, as_of)`
- Decisions: Decisions log rows dated 2026-10-26, stage 05 (C1, D1–D11 with D1a/D4a/D8a/D10a, R1, review fix pass)
- Collisions with the overall plan: none with the roadmap or spec; C1 between the Stage 05 and Stage 07 briefs (bar availability = session close + lag)
- Deviations from brief: none in scope. Additions: `market_date`, `is_early_close`, `to_available_at`, `AVAILABILITY_RULES`, the `calendar` config section, the `sim_clock` fixture, the AST string-form scan
- Known gaps → backlog: four review minors (see Backlog, "Stage 05"); D11 (ULID/structlog wall clock) stays open
- Next stage should know: every stored table's `available_at` must load as a numpy-backed `DatetimeTZDtype` column (`datetime64[ns|us, UTC]`); a pyarrow `timestamp[tz]` (`ArrowDtype`) column or a naive one is rejected by `pit_filter` with `DataError`, so read Parquet without `dtype_backend="pyarrow"` or convert. Stage 07 sets bar `available_at` with `bar_available_at(day, cal)` (16:15 ET, 13:15 ET on early closes) and SEC/news/FRED timestamps with `to_available_at(ts)`. Always build `TradingCalendar(config=cfg.calendar)` / `AsOf.for_session(..., config=cfg.calendar)` from the loaded config; the bare constructors use the built-in defaults. Code never reads the wall clock: get time from `get_clock()`; tests use the `sim_clock` fixture; `ruff check .` fails on `datetime.now()`/`date.today()`/`time.time()` etc., and `test_no_string_clock_reads_outside_clock_module` fails on `pd.Timestamp("now")`-style strings. pandas is 3.x (string dtype default, strict format inference in `to_datetime`). In this sandbox, a new worktree needs `UV_PROJECT_ENVIRONMENT=.venv.nosync uv sync --group dev` + `ln -s .venv.nosync .venv` and `UV_CACHE_DIR="$TMPDIR/uv-cache"` (and PyPI reachable for the sync)

- Key interfaces: `DEFAULT_REGISTRY.register(TableSchema(name=..., columns=(Column(name=..., dtype=ColumnType.X, nullable=False), ...), primary_key=(...), partition_by=(...), year_column=None, version=1, static=False))`; `lake = Lake(root, registry=None)` → `raw_path(vendor, dataset)`, `table_path(name)`, `table_files(name)`, `has_table(name)`, `table_schema(name)`, `write_table(name, df, partition_cols=None, mode="append"|"overwrite"|"upsert", keys=None) -> WriteResult(table, mode, rows, partitions)`, `read_table(name, filters=[(col, op, value)], columns=[...]) -> DataFrame` (sorted by primary key), `migrate(name) -> int`, `generation`; `registry.add_migration(name, from_version, fn)`; `db = Database(lake, read_only=False)` / `get_database(lake)` → `query(sql, params)` (one `SELECT`, `?` or `$name`), `refresh()`, `mirror_audit_log(path) -> int`, `close()`
- Decisions: Decisions log rows dated 2026-10-27, stage 06 (execution, collisions check, D1–D12, self-review fix pass, audit follow-up commit, log-key fix)
- Post-DONE audit (owner request): plan vs. work recorded in the [stage-06 worklog](stages/stage-06-storage/worklog.md); its one real gap (no test of the info log lines) was fixed in the follow-up commit, which also fixed two log keys clobbered by the run context
- Collisions with the overall plan: none (two points checked: parameterless DuckDB DDL, persisted relation views; both resolved by D7)
- Deviations from brief: none in scope. Additions: `static` tables, `year_column`/virtual `year` partition, schema fingerprint in `_meta.json`, `refresh()`, `read_only` mode, `get_database`, `to_pandas`, `atomic_write`
- Known gaps → backlog: list/`Decimal` column types; multi-partition atomicity; `SELECT` table functions can read any path; nanobind shutdown messages; untested dependency floors; run-context log-key clash (see Backlog, "Stage 06")
- Next stage should know: register curated tables in `DEFAULT_REGISTRY` at import time (e.g. `atlas.data.prices`); every non-static table needs `available_at` as a non-nullable `ColumnType.TIMESTAMP` column, and frames must already carry the right dtypes: tz-aware `datetime64` for timestamps (stored as UTC microseconds; finer precision is rejected), `datetime.date` objects or naive midnight `datetime64` for dates, real integer/float/bool/string dtypes (strings are never parsed into numbers or dates). Re-ingest with `mode="upsert"` (idempotent; the batch's last row per key wins). Partition columns must be primary-key columns (or `year` via `year_column`). Reads return `datetime64[us, UTC]` timestamps (numpy-backed, so `pit_filter` accepts them) and `datetime.date` dates; `Database.query` returns the same types. A schema change needs a version bump plus `registry.add_migration(...)` and `lake.migrate(name)`; reads and writes refuse a version or fingerprint mismatch. Never pass `run_id`, `mode`, `as_of` or `stage` as log keys: the run context overwrites them (use e.g. `write_mode`, `mirrored_run_id`). Tests: `tmp_lake` gives a lake with its own copy of `DEFAULT_REGISTRY`; close every `Database` you open (`with Database(lake) as db:`). Do not open a read-write and a read-only `Database` on the same file in one process at the same time (duckdb refuses a second configuration). In this sandbox, a new worktree needs `UV_PROJECT_ENVIRONMENT=.venv.nosync uv sync --group dev` + `ln -s .venv.nosync .venv` and `UV_CACHE_DIR="$TMPDIR/uv-cache"` (and PyPI reachable for the sync)

- Folder: [stages/stage-07-price-data/](stages/stage-07-price-data/) (`plan.md`, `worklog.md`, `verification.md`, `artifacts/`)
- Built: `atlas.data.providers` — `base.py` (`PriceProvider` protocol, `DAILY_COLUMNS`/`ACTION_COLUMNS`, `normalize_daily/actions`, `empty_daily/actions`, `check_request`, `RetryPolicy`, `call_with_retry`, `Throttle`), `yfinance_provider.py` (`YFinanceProvider`), `alpaca_provider.py` (`AlpacaPriceProvider`), `fallback.py` (`FallbackPriceProvider`, `build_price_provider`), `cache.py` (`CachedPriceProvider`); `atlas.data.prices` (`PRICES_DAILY`, `CORPORATE_ACTIONS`, `IngestReport`, `AdjustmentMismatch`, `ingest_prices`, `adjustment_factors`, `cross_check_adjustment`); `PriceDataConfig` (`cfg.data.prices`, `config/fund.yaml › data.prices`). 95 new tests (+2 live, deselected) in `tests/unit/data/test_price_*.py` with `price_helpers.py`; fixtures in `tests/fixtures/prices/` (README). Runtime deps yfinance 1.7.0, httpx 0.28.1; dev dep respx 0.23.1
- Key interfaces: `provider.fetch_daily(symbols, start, end)` / `fetch_actions(...)` → contract frames (both bounds inclusive; raw OHLC USD, raw `volume` int64, vendor `adj_close`; actions `type ∈ {split, dividend}`, `ratio` new/old, raw `amount`); `build_price_provider(cfg)` → yfinance, or a `FallbackPriceProvider` with Alpaca when both keys are set; `CachedPriceProvider(provider, lake.cache_dir / "prices")` (needs an installed clock; passes cached bars to a `ServedRecorder` such as `FallbackPriceProvider.record_served`); `ingest_prices(lake, provider, symbols, start, end, *, calendar=TradingCalendar(config=cfg.calendar), config=cfg.data.prices) -> IngestReport(rows, action_rows, symbols_ok, symbols_failed, failure_reasons, mismatches)`; tables `prices_daily` (PK symbol+date, partitioned by year) and `corporate_actions` (PK symbol+ex_date+type)
- Decisions: Decisions log rows dated 2026-10-27, stage 07 (execution, collisions check, D1–D10, Stage 06 fixture change, fixture provenance)
- Collisions with the overall plan: none (two interpretations recorded: cache from Goal/spec §16; actions `available_at` = ex-date bar)
- Corrected tests (Rule 1.6): `test_batches_and_throttles` expected 1 throttle pause before it ever passed; the test was wrong (split-history lookups are vendor requests too: 4 requests → 3 pauses), fixed in Task 2. `test_split_history_missing_for_traded_symbol_raises` pinned a deviation from plan D2 and was replaced by `test_symbol_without_split_history_is_dropped_and_logged` in the audit follow-up
- Post-DONE audit (owner request): plan vs work recorded in the [stage-07 worklog](stages/stage-07-price-data/worklog.md) (9 findings); items 1, 4, 8, 9 fixed and records 2, 5, 6 corrected in the follow-up commit; items 3 (planned `conftest.py` not created) and 7 (red step = collection error) left as recorded
- Deviations from brief: none in scope. Additions: `CachedPriceProvider` (Goal "caching"), `build_price_provider`, `vendor` column on `corporate_actions`, `IngestReport.action_rows`/`failure_reasons`, `adjustment_factors`/`cross_check_adjustment` as public functions, `PriceDataConfig`. Changed a Stage 06 test fixture (isolated registry; no assertion touched)
- Known gaps → backlog: Alpaca verified only on built fixtures (live test skipped, no keys); IEX vs consolidated volume; `adj_close` not point-in-time; unmapped action kinds and capital gains; no announcement dates; raw payloads not archived; unbounded per-instance memo (see Backlog, "Stage 07")
- Next stage should know: importing `atlas.data.prices` registers `prices_daily` and `corporate_actions` in `DEFAULT_REGISTRY` (so a test that registers its own schema under those names must use its own `SchemaRegistry`, as the storage tests now do). Prices in the lake are **raw**; use `adjustment_factors(daily, actions)` for split-adjusted (`split_adj_close`) or total-return (`calc_adj_close`) series, never the vendor `adj_close` for point-in-time work. Symbols are canonical (`BRK.B`); providers leave out symbols they have no data for (delisted, or yfinance bars without a split history: warning `prices.no_split_history`) instead of raising, and raise `DataError` only for vendor failures; `ingest_prices` never raises for one bad symbol (see `report.symbols_failed` / `failure_reasons`). Stage 08's delisted-name handling can use `report.symbols_failed` (reason "no price data returned"). yfinance un-adjustment needs the full split history (`yf.Ticker(s).splits`), so every symbol with rows costs one extra request; any exception inside a yfinance call becomes a chained `DataError` (read `__cause__`); `CachedPriceProvider` must run under `use_clock(LiveClock())` / `use_clock(SimClock(...))`; `batch_size`/throttle/retries come from `cfg.data.prices`. Tests: `tests/unit/data/price_helpers.py` has `FakePriceProvider`, `daily_rows`, `FakeYFinance`, `load_yf_symbol`; live tests are `@pytest.mark.live`. In this sandbox, a new worktree needs `UV_PROJECT_ENVIRONMENT=.venv.nosync uv sync --group dev` + `ln -s .venv.nosync .venv` and `UV_CACHE_DIR="$TMPDIR/uv-cache"`; Yahoo is reachable through the proxy via yfinance (bare curl gets 429)

No new dependencies
- Key interfaces: `cik_map = CikMap.from_company_tickers(client.company_tickers())`; `with build_edgar_client(cfg, lake) as client: ingest_fundamentals(lake, client, symbols, cik_map=cik_map, config=cfg) -> FundamentalsIngestReport(rows, symbols_ok, symbols_failed, failure_reasons, fallback_rows)`; `Fundamentals(lake, cfg).pit(symbol, as_of: date | AsOf) -> pd.Series` (index `metric_names(concept_map)`: `revenue_ttm, gross_profit_ttm, operating_income_ttm, net_income_ttm, eps_diluted_ttm, shares_outstanding, total_assets, total_liabilities, stockholders_equity, cash_and_equivalents, total_debt, operating_cash_flow_ttm, capex_ttm, rnd_ttm, sbc_ttm, gross_margin, operating_margin, roe, roa, debt_to_equity, fcf_ttm`; USD, USD/share, shares, ratios as fractions; NaN when unavailable) and `.pit_detail(...)` (`metric, value, period_start, period_end, available_at, accn, tag, source`); frame level `fundamentals_pit(facts, as_of, concept_map, config)`; quarter history `quarterly_values(resolve_concept(known_facts(facts, as_of), concept_map["revenue"]), cfg.data.fundamentals)`; table `fundamentals_facts` (PK symbol, taxonomy, tag, unit, period_end, duration_days, accn; partitioned by symbol)
- Decisions: Decisions log rows dated 2026-10-28, stage 09 (execution, collisions/interpretations, D1–D11, fixtures, execution changes, `call_with_retry` event, live test skipped)

- Gap-closing follow-up (owner request): `ingest_fundamentals` now validates the request before any download (bare string / invalid symbol → `ValueError`; case variants de-duplicated), test fixture without private access, verbose stage log, `make check` run on merged `main`; 3 new tests (suite 934 → 937)
- Owner-approved addendum: former members since 2018 get verified CIKs from `config/cik_overrides.yaml` (`build_cik_map(client, cfg)` = real `company_tickers.json` + overrides; use it instead of `CikMap.from_company_tickers` for ingestion); bank revenue falls back to net interest + noninterest income; suite 948
- Collisions with the overall plan: none (spec "keyed by filing date" → acceptance time with filed + 1 session fallback; `Fundamentals(lake).pit` for the brief's `fundamentals_pit(symbol, as_of)`; a fifth module `ingest.py`)
- Deviations from brief: none in scope. Additions: `ingest.py`, `pit_detail` (sources for every number), `taxonomy`/`duration_days`/`accepted_at` columns, staleness cut (`max_staleness_days`), `quarterly_values` public
- Known gaps → backlog: concept-map coverage; TTM EPS and average balances; 8-K items for Stage 12; per-call partition read (see Backlog, "Stage 09")
- Next stage should know: importing `atlas.data.fundamentals` registers `fundamentals_facts` in `DEFAULT_REGISTRY`. `available_at` = EDGAR acceptance time (UTC, verified) or, if a filing is missing from the index, the open of the first session after its filing date. Restatements are point-in-time: `known_facts` keeps, per tag and period, the latest filing known at `as_of`. The concept map path (`config/xbrl_map.yaml`) is relative to the working directory; `test_every_yaml_key_has_a_comment` covers every `config/*.yaml`, so write new YAML keys with a comment above (flow style keeps it short). `build_edgar_client` needs `SEC_USER_AGENT` in `.env` (never log it); www.sec.gov (company_tickers.json) answers 403 without contact details, data.sec.gov accepted the descriptive User-Agent on 2026-10-28. Stage 10 (FRED/ALFRED) can mirror this package's shape: client with throttle/retries/raw cache, parser to a long table with `available_at` = vintage release time, `latest_filings`-style "latest vintage known at `as_of`". In this sandbox, a new worktree needs `UV_PROJECT_ENVIRONMENT=.venv.nosync uv sync --group dev` + `ln -s .venv.nosync .venv` and `UV_CACHE_DIR="$TMPDIR/uv-cache"`; the main checkout is not writable, so merging into `main` needs the owner's fast-forward (Rule 6 exception)

- Folder: [stages/stage-10-macro/](stages/stage-10-macro/) (`plan.md`, `worklog.md`, `verification.md`, `artifacts/`)

- Key interfaces: `with build_fred_client(cfg, lake) as client: ingest_macro(lake, client, series_ids=None, *, config=cfg, observation_end=None, realtime_end=None) -> MacroIngestReport(rows, series_ok, series_failed, failure_reasons, revision_rows)`; `Macro(lake, cfg).pit(series_ids, as_of: date | AsOf, lookback: timedelta) -> DataFrame` (index `obs_date` tz-naive `DatetimeIndex`, one float column per id in request order, FRED units); `Macro.pit_detail(...)` (rows used, with `realtime_start`, `available_at`); `Macro.series_info()`; transforms take a column of that frame and return percent / percentage points (`zscore` unitless)
- Decisions: Decisions log rows dated 2026-10-29, stage 10 (execution, interpretations incl. the market-timing flag, D1–D9, fixtures, secret handling)
- Collisions with the overall plan: none (interpretations only, see the Decisions log)
- Deviations from brief: none in scope. Additions: `macro_series` metadata table, `pit_detail`, `ingest.py`/`series.py`, configurable market timing, raw archive
- Known gaps → backlog: credit spreads only from 2023-10-30; market-series timing choice; scheduled (not actual) release times; live test pending `FRED_API_KEY`; per-call read (see Backlog, "Stage 10")
- Next stage should know: importing `atlas.data.macro` registers `macro_observations` and `macro_series` in `DEFAULT_REGISTRY` and adds a key-masking filter to the `httpx`/`httpcore` loggers. Series ids must be in `config/macro.yaml` (path relative to the working directory). An observation dated the 1st of a month stands for that month (monthly) — choose `lookback` accordingly. In this worktree setup (iCloud-synced Desktop) run commands with `UV_NO_SYNC=1 PYTHONPATH=src`: the venv's editable `.pth` gets hidden. Copy the root `.env` into a new worktree for live tests Market series (yields, spreads, VIX) for day D are known at 06:00 ET on D + 1 (owner-approved; the brief said 08:00), release series at their `release_time` on the vintage date.

- Key interfaces: `ingest_news(lake, build_news_provider(cfg), symbols | None, start, end) -> NewsIngestReport(rows, rows_by_provider)` (aware datetimes, `[start, end)`); `News(lake, cfg).pit(symbol, as_of: date | AsOf, lookback_days=None, limit=None) -> list[Evidence]` (`Evidence.id = "news:<article_id>"`, `as_of` = publish time UTC, `text` = headline + blank line + summary); `News.pit_detail(...)` (frame with `relevance`, `headline_match`); `News.market(as_of, ...)` (untagged or SPY/QQQ-tagged, newest first); `News.articles(start, end)`

Rulings: Yahoo uses the search API; Yahoo keeps only tagged results; no `config` on `ingest_news`; Yahoo-only with a warning when Alpaca keys are missing
- Known gaps → backlog: Alpaca live test and real fixtures pending keys (news history needs them); edited text leaks; per-call partition reads; Yahoo retry breadth; `pd.NA` list cells; `get_news` 404 (see Backlog, "Stage 11")
- Next stage should know: importing `atlas.data.news` registers `news` in `DEFAULT_REGISTRY`. Article ids are `<provider>-<vendor id>`; cite `news:<article_id>`. Duplicates and roundups are hidden at query time, not in the table, so read news through `News` (or `news_pit*`), not the raw table. Candidates for a ticker are the vendor's tags. Without Alpaca keys, `build_news_provider` returns Yahoo (recent only) and warns. In this sandbox run `uv` with `UV_CACHE_DIR=$TMPDIR/uv-cache UV_NO_SYNC=1`

Gap follow-up: `ingest_macro_calendar(lake, fred | None, *, config=None, known_at=None)` (clock default); `ingest_upcoming_events(lake, provider, symbols, *, known_at=None)` (alias `ingest_upcoming_earnings`; earnings and ex-dividend rows); `upcoming` rows may have `source` `estimated` (and `event_type` `ex_dividend` from `yahoo`); `upcoming_events(view, as_of, horizon_days, *, scheduled_match_days, estimate_min_gap_days=None)`; `Lexicon.for_year(year)`; `ingest_social_posts(lake, providers, symbols, start, end) -> SocialIngestReport`, `Social(lake, cfg).evidence(symbol, as_of, lookback_days=None, limit=None)`

Estimated earnings rows (`source` `estimated`) are estimates: show them as such. `Sentiment` needs the lexicon file (`download_lm_lexicon(cfg.data.sentiment)` once; it is in `data/reference/` here, gitignored) and the `news` table. `ingest_macro_calendar` reads `config/macro_calendar.yaml` relative to the working directory. Upcoming earnings need regular `ingest_upcoming_earnings` runs to build snapshot history (Stage 14). Reactions need `prices_daily` for the symbol and SPY. In this sandbox run `uv` with `UV_CACHE_DIR=$TMPDIR/uv-cache UV_NO_SYNC=1`; EDGAR occasionally drops TLS connections from the sandbox (retried; re-run)

- Folder: [stages/stage-13-data-quality-dataview/](stages/stage-13-data-quality-dataview/) (`plan.md`, `worklog.md`, `verification.md`, `artifacts/`)

- Key interfaces: `quality_report(lake, as_of: date | AsOf, *, config=None, calendar=None, concept_map=None, persist=False) -> DataFrame[ISSUE_COLUMNS]` (`table, symbol, date, check, severity, detail, available_at`); `blocked_symbols(report) -> frozenset[str]`; `read_quality_issues(lake, as_of)`; `DataView(lake, as_of: date | AsOf, config=None, *, calendar=None, concept_map=None, catalog=None, lexicon=None)` with `.as_of`, `.day`, `.config`, `.advance(new_as_of)`, `.prices(symbols, lookback_sessions)` (raw OHLCV USD/shares + our PIT `adj_close`), `.returns(symbols, lookback_sessions)` (wide, simple daily total returns, `DatetimeIndex` `date`), `.adv(symbols, lookback=None)` (`adv_usd`, `adv_shares` split-adjusted, `sessions`; default `limits.adv_window_days`), `.fundamentals(symbol) -> Series`, `.fundamentals_panel(symbols) -> DataFrame`, `.macro(series_ids, lookback: timedelta | days)`, `.news(symbol, lookback=None, limit=None) -> list[Evidence]`, `.market_news(...)`, `.sentiment(symbol) -> SentimentAggregate`, `.sentiment_daily(symbol, days=None)`, `.events(symbols=None, horizon_days=None)`, `.past_events(symbol, n=None)`, `.earnings_reactions(symbol, n=None)`, `.social(symbol, lookback=None, limit=None)`, `.universe(name="sp500")`, `.tradable(name="sp500")`, `.instrument(symbol) -> Instrument`, `.quality_issues()`, `.blocked`, `.cache_info()`
- Decisions: Decisions log rows dated 2026-10-30, stage 13 (execution, interpretations 1–8, D1–D8, rulings, self-review)
- Collisions with the overall plan: none (interpretations only, see the Decisions log)
- Deviations from brief: none in scope. Additions: `vendor_mix` check, `QualityIssue.available_at`, DataView methods beyond the brief's list (see interpretation 7), `cache_info`. Process: Task 12's `cache_info` written together with its tests; the doc presence test written after the doc section
- Known gaps → backlog: sync should persist daily reports (Stage 14); report recomputed per as-of (Stage 56); whole-table rewrite on persist; per-symbol `fundamentals_panel`; unrecorded splits outside the ratio list; block window vs 252-day features (see Backlog, "Stage 13")
- Next stage should know: strategy and agent code must read data only through `DataView` (it imports every data package, so all tables are registered). A `date` as-of uses `cfg.calendar.decision_time` (08:00 ET): the as-of day's own bar is not known. Lookbacks: prices/returns/adv in sessions, news/social in calendar days, macro a `timedelta` or calendar days. A blocked symbol is missing from multi-symbol results and raises `DataError` from single-symbol methods; check `view.blocked` / `view.quality_issues()` to say why. `prices()["adj_close"]` is ours (actions known at `as_of`, relative to the last bar in the window), not the vendor's. The view never writes; to store a day's issues call `quality_report(lake, day, persist=True)` (Stage 14's sync). `DataView` needs the concept map, macro catalog and lexicon files (relative paths from config) unless passed in. Tests: `tests/unit/data/view_helpers.py` has `build_world(lake, future=...)` (a clean and a dirty lake for leak tests), `make_bars`, `make_actions`, `make_facts`, `macro_rows`. In this sandbox run `uv` with `UV_CACHE_DIR=$TMPDIR/uv-cache UV_NO_SYNC=1`

- Built: `atlas.quant.features` — `registry.py` (`FeatureSpec`, `FeatureRegistry`, `REGISTRY`, `@feature`, `PricePanel`, `compute_panel`, `compute_features`, `normalize_symbols`), `technical.py` (40 features: `ret_{1,5,21,63,126,252}d`, `momentum_12_1`, `reversal_5d`, `vol_21d`, `vol_63d`, `downside_vol_63d`, `atr_14`, `atr_pct_14`, `rsi_14`, `macd`, `macd_signal`, `macd_hist`, `bb_pctb_20`, `sma_/ema_{20,50,200}`, `price_to_sma_/price_to_ema_{20,50,200}`, `dist_52w_high`, `dist_52w_low`, `max_dd_252d`, `beta_252d`, `idio_vol_252d`, `volume_z_21d`, `amihud_21d`, `gap_1d`, `gap_mean_21d`, `gap_std_63d`; smoothers `smooth`/`ema`/`wilder`), `snapshot.py` (`FeatureReading`, `TechnicalSnapshot`, `build_snapshots`, `percentile_rank`); config `cfg.quant.features` (`QuantConfig`/`FeaturesConfig`/`SnapshotConfig`, YAML in `config/fund.yaml`); `DataView.sessions(lookback)`; test world `tests/tiny_universe.py` + fixture `tiny_universe`; reference `tests/unit/quant/features/feature_reference.py` + `tests/fixtures/features/{ohlcv,expected}.csv`
- Key interfaces: `compute_features(view, symbols, names=None, *, history=1, layout="wide"|"long", registry=REGISTRY)` → `{name: DataFrame[date × symbol]}` or a `(date, symbol) × feature` frame for the last `history` known sessions; `compute_panel(panel, names)` (pure); `build_snapshots(view, symbols, *, names=None, universe=None) -> dict[str, TechnicalSnapshot]` (`.reading(name)`, `.value(name)`; `pct_own` / `pct_universe` fractions); `REGISTRY.get(name)` → `lookback`, `kind`, `unit`, `description`, `comparable`
- Decisions: Decisions log rows dated 2026-11-02, stage 15 (execution, interpretations 1–8, D1–D7, the Stage 13 `DataView.sessions` change and `tiny_universe`, the Tasks 7–13 process deviation)
- Collisions with the overall plan: none (interpretations only)
- Deviations from brief: none in scope or criteria; reference values come from an independent Python implementation (the brief allowed "hand-computed or TA-Lib-reference"); tolerance rtol 1e-6 with an absolute floor 1e-9
- Known gaps → backlog: old bad bars inside long windows; recursive features depend on the window start (keep `buffer_sessions` equal in backtest and live); no feature cache; dollar-volume (not share) volume features; no `xs` features (see Backlog, "Stage 15")
- Next stage should know: importing `atlas.quant.features` registers the 40 features. Feature names follow Stage 17's example (`momentum_12_1`). Units are in `FeatureSpec.unit` (`fraction`, `annual_fraction`, `usd`, `ratio`, `index_0_100`, `zscore`, `return_per_usd_mm`); USD features are not comparable across symbols. Rows are trading sessions (`DataView.sessions`): a missing bar is NaN and window features stay NaN until the gap leaves the window; blocked symbols are absent; a missing or blocked benchmark gives NaN betas. Results are float64 with no infinities. Tests can use the `tiny_universe` fixture (5 tickers × 300 sessions with known betas 0.5/1.0/1.3/1.8, `sp500` membership) and `add_future_rows` from `tests/tiny_universe.py` for leak tests; test basenames must be unique across `tests/`. In this sandbox run `uv` with `UV_CACHE_DIR=$TMPDIR/uv-cache-s15 UV_NO_SYNC=1`

### Stage 16 — Fundamental factor library — DONE 2026-11-04
- Folder: [stages/stage-16-fundamental-factors/](stages/stage-16-fundamental-factors/) (`plan.md`, `worklog.md`, `verification.md`, `artifacts/`)
- Built: `atlas.quant.factors` — `fundamental.py` (`FactorSpec`, `FACTORS` (17 raw factors in 6 groups: value `earnings_yield`, `book_yield`, `fcf_yield`, `ebit_ev`, `sales_ev`; quality `roe`, `roa`, `gross_profitability`, `accruals` (negated), `margin_stability` (−std); growth `revenue_growth`, `eps_growth`, `revenue_cagr_3y`; leverage `debt_to_equity`, `net_debt_ebitda` (EBIT); `size`; issuance `net_issuance` (negated)), `adjusted_shares`, `compute_market`, `raw_factors`, `FactorPanel`, `factor_panel`, `factor_config_hash`), `cleaning.py` (`winsorize`, `zscore(basis=)`, `fill_missing`, `neutralize`, `clean_factors`, `CleanedFactors`), `composite.py` (`composite`, `style_composites`), `store.py` (`FACTOR_EXPOSURES` table `factor_exposures`, `FactorStore`, `StoredExposures`, `monthly_rebalance_dates`, `build_factor_cache`); config `cfg.quant.factors` (`FactorsConfig`, YAML in `config/fund.yaml`); Stage 09 `ttm_values`, `concept_history` (+ `HISTORY_COLUMNS`, `TTM_COLUMNS`); Stage 13 `DataView.fundamentals_history`, `DataView.split_factors`; test world `tests/unit/quant/factors/factor_world.py`
- Key interfaces: `factor_panel(view, symbols=None, *, config=None) -> FactorPanel` (`.raw`, `.exposures` (z-scores), `.filled` (bool), `.market` (`price`, `shares`, `market_cap`, `enterprise_value`, USD), `.sectors`, `.config_hash`); `composite(factors, weights, *, standardize=True)`; `style_composites(exposures)` → one column per group; `FactorStore(lake, config).write(panel)` / `.read(as_of) -> StoredExposures | None` / `.dates()`; `build_factor_cache(lake, monthly_rebalance_dates(start, end), config=cfg)`
- Decisions: Decisions log rows dated 2026-11-04, stage 16 (execution, collisions/interpretations, D1–D8, earlier-stage changes, the audit fix for sector-less names, the two small audit fixes)
- Collisions with the overall plan: none; one deviation in an In-scope item (net debt / EBIT instead of EBITDA: no D&A in the concept map)
- Audit (owner's request, 2026-11-04): every brief item and plan task checked; found that all 357 former S&P 500 members have no sector, so grouping sector-less names leaked future index exits → fixed (scored against the whole cross-section); also fixed a hard-coded quarter span and a silent fallback when no instruments row exists; a real-lake crash (empty-quarter dtype in Stage 09 `_to_dates`) found by the smoke run and fixed
- Known gaps → backlog: EBITDA (needs D&A + re-ingest); sector backfill for former members (Stage 08); thin raw coverage of gross-profit and EV-based factors (filled + flagged); 41 names without a market cap; multi-class share counts; panel speed (~2–3 min per real-lake date) and no cache CLI; suite runtime ~3 min (see Backlog, "Stage 16")
- Next stage should know: `factor_panel` reads only through `DataView`; exposures are z-scores over the names with a known sector (each sector sums to 0; optional size neutralisation in `quant.factors.size_neutral`); names without a sector are scored against the whole market; use `.filled` before trusting a factor with low raw coverage; `market_cap` is price × split-adjusted cover-page shares (USD), for √mcap weights (Stage 19). Raw factor units are in `FactorSpec.unit`. Cached panels: `FactorStore.read(as_of)` returns the latest stored month-end on or before the as-of with the current `config_hash`; the real lake's cache has not been filled yet. Tests can build the synthetic world with `build_factor_world(lake)` (12 companies, 16 quarters, known growth and share drift, a CCC split). In this sandbox run `uv` with `UV_CACHE_DIR=$TMPDIR/uv-cache-s16 UV_NO_SYNC=1`

### Stage 17 — Signal research toolkit (IC, quantiles, decay) — DONE 2026-11-04
- Folder: [stages/stage-17-signal-research/](stages/stage-17-signal-research/) (`plan.md`, `worklog.md`, `verification.md`, `artifacts/` incl. the real-lake tear sheet `tearsheet-momentum_12_1/`)
- Built: `atlas.quant.research` — `labels.py` (`compound_forward`, `ForwardReturns`, `forward_returns`, `check_dates`, `check_horizons`), `signals.py` (`evaluation_dates`, `signal_kind`, `SignalPanel`, `signal_panel`), `ic.py` (`ic_series`, `newey_west_tstat`, `session_step`, `nw_lags`, `ICSummary`, `summarize_ic`, `ic_by_group`, `ic_by_regime`, `ic_decay`, `rank_autocorrelation`), `quantiles.py` (`quantile_buckets`, `quantile_returns`, `monotonicity`, `held_rows`, `daily_quantile_returns`, `QuantileAnalysis`, `quantile_analysis`), `vector_backtest.py` (`rebalance_dates`, `long_short_weights`, `BacktestResult`, `vector_backtest`), `tearsheet.py` (`SignalTearSheet`, `build_tearsheet`, `research_signal`, `FIGURES`); CLI `atlas research signal` (`src/atlas/cli/research_cmd.py`); config `cfg.quant.research` (`ResearchConfig`, `ResearchBacktestConfig`; YAML in `config/fund.yaml`); label tag + guard in `atlas.core.pit` (`LABEL_ATTR`, `tag_label`, `is_label`, `guard_not_label`), also called by Stage 16's `composite`; new runtime dependency matplotlib ≥ 3.11; test world `tests/unit/quant/research/research_world.py`
- Key interfaces: `forward_returns(view_factory, dates, symbols, horizons=None, *, config=None) -> ForwardReturns` (`.horizon(h)`, `.daily`, `.known_at`, `.blocked`); `signal_panel(name, view_factory, dates, *, universe=None) -> SignalPanel` (`.values` dates × symbols, `.members`, `.sectors`); `ic_series(signal, labels, *, method="spearman")`; `quantile_analysis(signal, labels, horizon)`; `vector_backtest(signal, daily)`; `research_signal(name, view_factory, start, end, *, config, frequency, universe, regimes) -> SignalTearSheet` (`.summary`, `.save(dir)`); `view_factory(day) -> DataView`
- Decisions: Decisions log rows dated 2026-11-04, stage 17 (execution, interpretations 1–13, D1–D8)
- Collisions with the overall plan: none. Interpretations: `forward_returns` also takes dates and symbols; a sixth file `signals.py`; matplotlib added (the brief requires it)
- Known gaps → backlog: one label view per run (a name blocked on that day loses its labels; none in the real run); no delisting proceeds; no borrow/financing cost in the vectorised backtest; ~38 min per 72 monthly dates on the real lake (fundamental factors ~3 h without the Stage 16 cache); CLI lacks `--horizons`/`--regimes` options (see Backlog, "Stage 17")
- Next stage should know: timing is decision day `t` (08:00, bars to `t−1`) → label close(`t`) → close(`t+h`); the backtest trades at the close of `t` and earns from `t+1`. Labels are tagged: passing them (or anything derived with pandas ops) as a signal raises `LookAheadError`; IC series and quantile outputs are not tagged but are dated at the signal date and only known after the horizon, so lag them before using them as weights (Stage 22). By-sector IC leaves names without a sector out (the former members, Stage 16 lesson). Regimes plug in through `regimes=` (Stage 21). Real-lake result: `momentum_12_1` had a small negative IC on S&P 500 names 2016–2021 (21-session Spearman −0.040, NW t −1.57; L/S Sharpe −0.29). Run the CLI on the main lake with `ATLAS__DATA__LAKE_PATH=~/Projects/atlas-capital/data` and `--out` (the main checkout is not writable from the sandbox); set `MPLCONFIGDIR=$TMPDIR/mpl` in the sandbox

### Stage 18 — Covariance & volatility forecasting — DONE 2026-11-04
- Folder: [stages/stage-18-covariance-vol/](stages/stage-18-covariance-vol/) (`plan.md`, `worklog.md`, `verification.md`, `artifacts/` incl. `mutate.py`, `real_lake_check.py`)
- Built: `atlas.quant.risk_model` — `psd.py` (`is_psd`, `condition_number`, `nearest_psd` (Higham 1988; `keep_diagonal=True` → Higham 2002), `nearest_correlation`, `floor_condition`), `covariance.py` (`CovResult`, `CovarianceEstimator`, `SampleCovariance`, `EWMACovariance`, `LedoitWolfCovariance` (constant-correlation target), `OASCovariance`, `covariance_estimator`, `corr_from_cov`, `cov_from_corr`, `METHODS`), `volatility.py` (`annualise_vol`, `annualise_variance`, `deannualise_vol`, `ewma_lambda`, `ewma_variance_path`, `VolForecast`, `ewma_vol`, `realised_vol`, `garch_vol`, `blend_vol`, `forecast_vol`, `forecast_vols`, `VOL_METHODS`); config `cfg.quant.risk_model` (`RiskModelConfig`, `VolatilityConfig`, `BlendWeights`; YAML in `config/fund.yaml`); new runtime dependency `arch` ≥ 8.0 (brings scipy, statsmodels; imported lazily by the GARCH fit only); test world `tests/unit/quant/risk_model/risk_world.py`
- Key interfaces: `covariance_estimator(method=None, *, config=None, **kw).estimate(returns) -> CovResult` (`.cov`, `.corr` DataFrames and `.vol` Series are fresh copies; `.matrix` read-only; `.symbols`, `.method`, `.n_obs`, `.end`, `.shrinkage`, `.condition_number`, `.adjustments`, `.dropped` name → reason); `forecast_vol(returns: Series, method=None) -> VolForecast` (`.variance`, `.vol`, `.annual_vol()`, `.params`, `.fallback`); `forecast_vols(frame, method)` → dict (names without a forecast left out)
- Decisions: Decisions log rows dated 2026-11-04, stage 18 (execution, interpretations 1–10, D1–D5, fixes during the stage)
- Collisions with the overall plan: none. Interpretations: all outputs daily (annualise with `quant.features.sessions_per_year`); EWMA and vol forecasts zero-mean; LW/OAS divide by `n` as in their papers; pairwise default with nearest-PSD repair keeping variances
- Known gaps → backlog: no DataView convenience; one-step GARCH only; pairwise approximation in LW/OAS; `complete` all-or-nothing; N > T sample/EWMA always floored; `VolForecast` unhashable (see Backlog, "Stage 18")
- Next stage should know: input is a returns frame (`DataView.returns`), sessions ascending; names with < 63 returns, or < 63 common sessions with another name, or constant, are **dropped and listed in `CovResult.dropped`** (never estimated) — the factor model must handle names absent from the result. Every covariance is symmetric positive definite with condition number ≤ 1e5; prefer Ledoit-Wolf/OAS for 500 names on a 1-year window (real lake: LW cond 4.5e3, sample/EWMA singular and floored). GARCH falls back to EWMA (reason in `VolForecast.fallback`, `method="ewma"`); all forecasts raise `DataError` rather than return a zero or guessed vol. `CovResult` compares by identity (use `np.array_equal(a.matrix, b.matrix)`); `missing="complete"` keeps session positions (EWMA decay). Real-lake check: run with `ATLAS__DATA__LAKE_PATH=~/Projects/atlas-capital/data` (read only)

### Stage 19 — Factor risk model — DONE 2026-11-04
- Folder: [stages/stage-19-factor-risk-model/](stages/stage-19-factor-risk-model/) (`plan.md`, `worklog.md`, `verification.md`, `artifacts/` incl. `mutate.py`, `real_lake_check.py`, `real-lake-bias.txt`)
- Built: `atlas.quant.risk_model` — `regression.py` (`sector_constraint`, `constrained_wls`: null-space constrained WLS, `RegressionResult`), `specific.py` (`ewma_specific_variance`, `shrink_to_sector_median` (USE4 form), `specific_variance`, `SpecificResult`), `factor_model.py` (`FactorSet` (market, 11 GICS sectors, 8 styles = 20 factors), `standardize_styles`, `prepare_exposures`, `CrossSection`, `FactorHistory`, `fit_factor_history`, `newey_west_covariance`, `factor_covariance`, `RiskModel`, `build_risk_model`), `analytics.py` (`PortfolioRisk`, `portfolio_risk`, `factor_exposures`, `marginal_contribution`, `bias_statistic`), `etf_mapping.py` (`etf_exposures`, `EtfExposures`, `extend_model`), `store.py` (lake tables `factor_returns`, `risk_model_snapshots`; `RiskModelStore`, `risk_model_config_hash`), `loader.py` (`style_inputs`, `load_cross_sections`, `build_factor_history`, `build_model_from_view`, `etf_exposures_from_view`, `build_and_store`); config `cfg.quant.risk_model.factor_model` (`FactorModelConfig`, `EtfConfig`; YAML in `config/fund.yaml`); test worlds `tests/unit/quant/risk_model/factor_risk_world.py` (+ `conftest.py` reusing the Stage 16 lake world); integration test `tests/integration/test_risk_model_real_lake.py`
- Key interfaces: `fit_factor_history(cross_sections, FactorSet.from_config(fm), fm, on_short="raise"|"skip") -> FactorHistory`; `build_risk_model(history, raw_styles, sectors, caps, as_of, config) -> RiskModel` (`.cov(symbols=None)` = XFXᵀ + D daily, `.vol()`, `.exposures`, `.factor_cov`, `.specific_var` fresh copies, `.dropped` name → reason, `.adjustments`, `.n_days`, `.etfs`); `portfolio_risk(model, w)` (w = NAV fractions; nonzero weight on a name outside the model → `DataError`); real data: `build_model_from_view(view, history)`, `etf_exposures_from_view(view, history, etfs)`, `build_and_store(lake, store, start, end, config)`
- Decisions: Decisions log rows dated 2026-11-04, stage 19 (execution, interpretations 1–14, fixes during the stage, audit)
- Collisions with the overall plan: none. Interpretations: build-time criterion = build from a fitted history; `beta` is the brief's low-vol style (orientation never flipped); √cap regression weights make the market factor approximate (not equal) the cap-weighted return
- Known gaps → backlog: slow Stage 16 `factor_panel` (~136 s per as-of); no return truncation before the regression; anchors update the universe and fundamental styles only monthly (quarterly in the evidence run); names blocked today leave the whole loaded window; no CLI for `build_and_store`; ETF betas along the constraint direction not identified; **real-data coverage ~387 of ~503 members per day** (missing sectors / caps / delisted prices, Stages 07/08/09) (see Backlog, "Stage 19")
- Next stage should know: `RiskModel.as_of` is the **data date** (the last session included); a decision on the morning of D uses the model of D−1 (`RiskModelStore.read_snapshot(D)` returns it; `build_model_from_view(DataView(lake, D))` builds it). All numbers are daily (annualise with `annualise_vol`). Names without a sector, cap, enough styles or 63 residual days are dropped and listed, never guessed — check `model.dropped` and do not hold names outside `model.symbols`. A model needs factor returns within 5 sessions of its as-of. Real-lake builds are slow because of `factor_panel` (~2 min per as-of): reuse a stored snapshot or a fitted `FactorHistory` rather than rebuilding. Real-lake check: `ATLAS__DATA__LAKE_PATH=~/Projects/atlas-capital/data` (read only)

### Stage 20 — Risk & performance metrics library — DONE 2026-11-04
- Folder: [stages/stage-20-risk-metrics/](stages/stage-20-risk-metrics/) (`plan.md`, `worklog.md`, `verification.md`, `artifacts/` incl. `mutate.py`, `real_lake_check.py`, `empyrical_reference.py`)
- Built: `atlas.quant.metrics` — `performance.py` (`cumulative_return`, `annualised_return` (CAGR), `annualised_mean`, `annualised_vol`, `sharpe_ratio`, `sortino_ratio`, `downside_risk`, `calmar_ratio`, `omega_ratio`, `wealth_index`, `drawdown_series`, `max_drawdown` (positive), `drawdown_periods` / `DrawdownPeriod`, `max_drawdown_duration`, `ulcer_index`, `skewness`, `excess_kurtosis`, `hit_rate`, `profit_factor`, `tail_ratio`, `rolling_metric`, `rolling_sharpe`, `rolling_vol`, `rolling_max_drawdown`, `performance_summary`), `relative.py` (`beta`, `alpha`, `tracking_error`, `information_ratio`, `active_return`, `up_capture`, `down_capture`, `up_down_capture`, `correlation`, `rolling_beta`, `rolling_correlation`, `relative_summary`), `var.py` (`var_cvar` → `VaRResult`, `value_at_risk`, `conditional_value_at_risk`, `portfolio_var`, `portfolio_returns`, `simulate_portfolio_returns`, `normal_var/cvar`, `cornish_fisher_quantile/var/cvar`, `scale_to_horizon`), `contributions.py` (`risk_contributions` → `RiskContributions`), `liquidity.py` (`days_to_liquidate`, `liquidity_profile` → `LiquidityProfile`, `liquidity_from_view`), `exposure.py` (`exposure_summary` → `ExposureSummary`, `group_net`, `group_gross`, `beta_adjusted_net`, `UNCLASSIFIED`), `_common.py` (validation); config `cfg.quant.metrics` (`MetricsConfig`; YAML in `config/fund.yaml`); fixture `tests/fixtures/metrics/empyrical_reference.json`; Stage 17 `vector_backtest` summary now uses the library
- Key interfaces: every function takes daily simple returns (Series by session, NaN dropped) or weights (Series of NAV fractions) and returns floats in the units its docstring states; `periods` default `quant.features.sessions_per_year`; `risk_free` per session (scalar / Series / `None` = `quant.metrics.risk_free_annual`). `var_cvar(series, method, var_confidence, cvar_confidence, horizon, pnl)` (defaults `limits.var_confidence` 0.95, `limits.cvar_confidence` 0.975, `quant.metrics.var_method` historical); `portfolio_var(w, returns=… | cov=…, method, seed/rng)`; `risk_contributions(w, cov | RiskModel)`; `liquidity_profile(positions_usd, adv_usd)` / `liquidity_from_view(view, positions_usd)`; `exposure_summary(w, betas=, sectors=)`
- Decisions: Decisions log rows dated 2026-11-04, stage 20 (execution, interpretations, single source and audit)
- Collisions with the overall plan: none. Interpretations: not enough data → `DataError`, undefined ratio → NaN (never ±inf); VaR/CVaR positive losses; drawdowns from the initial NAV of 1 (Stage 17 behaviour change, test corrected)
- Known gaps → backlog: Monte Carlo is multivariate normal only; multi-day VaR by √h / overlapping (no GARCH term structure); risk-free default is a config scalar; Cornish-Fisher CVaR overshoots at high kurtosis on real data; historical-simulation VaR drops sessions with a missing held return (see Backlog, "Stage 20")
- Next stage should know: import every risk / performance number from `atlas.quant.metrics` (do not re-implement). VaR/CVaR are positive fractions of NAV, compare them directly with `limits.max_var` / `limits.max_cvar`; `max_drawdown` and `drawdown_series` follow `limits.drawdown_tiers` (positive magnitudes; current drawdown = `drawdown_series(r).iloc[-1]`). Functions raise `DataError` when data is short (VaR needs `quant.metrics.var_min_obs` = 63) — fail safe, never a number; Monte Carlo needs an explicit `seed` or `rng` and at least `var_min_obs` scenarios. `method="cornish_fisher"` raises `DataError` outside its domain (typical for daily equity returns, e.g. SPY) — use `historical`. `liquidity_from_view` treats an ADV over fewer than 90% of its window (`quant.metrics.adv_min_coverage`) as missing (`inf` days). Names without ADV get `inf` days; names without a sector go to `Unclassified` (7 of 50 in the real-lake book, Stage 19 coverage gap). Real-lake check: `ATLAS__DATA__LAKE_PATH=~/Projects/atlas-capital/data` (read only), runs in ~9 s

### Stage 22 — Systematic alpha models — DONE 2026-11-07
- Folder: [stages/stage-22-systematic-alpha/](stages/stage-22-systematic-alpha/) (`plan.md`, `worklog.md`, `verification.md`, `artifacts/` incl. `research_run.py`, `sign_label_check.py`, `mutate.py`, `research/` (run 1, pre-registered) and `research-respec/` (re-specified composite) with tear sheets and `summary.json`)

- Key interfaces: `AlphaModel.predict(view) -> DataFrame[symbol: score, exp_return_ann, confidence]` (index `symbol` sorted unique; `exp_return_ann` annualised simple return as a fraction — residual for the cross-sectional models, own return for TSMOM; `confidence` ∈ [0, 1]; a name without a view is absent, never NaN), checked by `check_alpha_frame`; `direction(frame)`; models `MultiFactorModel`, `TSMOMModel`, `PEADModel` with `name` / `horizon` (21 sessions); `grinold_alpha(z, ic, sigma_ann, horizon=, periods=)`
- Decisions: Decisions log rows dated 2026-11-07, stage 22 (execution, interpretations, failed pre-registered criterion, **owner decision "Re-specify in-sample" / "Re-specify and re-run"**)
- Collisions with the overall plan: none. Readings: the brief's `α = IC × σ_resid × z` holds at the IC's horizon; `exp_return_ann` multiplies by √(P/h)
- **In-sample fit, said plainly:** the pre-registered equal-weight composite failed (IC −0.0062, t −0.42). On the owner's decision the weights were refitted on the same 2016–2021 data, **after seeing the component ICs**: weight ∝ max(component IC_h21, 0) → quality 0.62, reversal 0.38, momentum / value / low-vol 0. Its in-sample IC (+0.0256, t +1.91) is chosen after seeing it and **overstates the real edge**; out-of-sample check in Stage 58 (Backlog). Variants scored: V1 equal −0.0062 (t −0.42); V2 fitted +0.0256 (+1.91); PIT IC-weighted diagnostics −0.024 / −0.017. Signs and label alignment checked on the real lake: no bug. Hold-out (2022+) never read

- Next stage should know: alpha frames are the hand-off to Stages 34 / 37 / 39 (consume `exp_return_ann` and `confidence`, never re-derive); the multi-factor weights live in `config/fund.yaml › quant.alpha.multifactor.components` and are an in-sample fit — do not tune them further on 2016–2021, and never on 2022+; `MultiFactorModel.predict_components(view, component_frame(view))` scores several weightings on one panel; on the real lake run long loops with `DataView.advance` (shared cache: ~15–30 s per monthly day vs ~200 s fresh)

### Stage 23 — Statistical arbitrage (pairs) — DONE 2026-11-09
- Folder: [stages/stage-23-stat-arb/](stages/stage-23-stat-arb/) (`plan.md`, `worklog.md`, `verification.md`, `artifacts/` incl. `insample_run.py` + `insample/`, `mutate.py`, `eg_power_probe.py`, `fp_rate_probe.py`, `fp_clustered_probe.py`, `recovery_rates.py`)
- Built: `atlas.quant.statarb` — `selection.py` (`pair_id`, `half_life`, `hurst_exponent`, `hedge_ratio`, `coint_pvalue` (order-free Engle-Granger), `PairStats`, `evaluate_pair`, `Pair`, `log_price_panel`, `select_pairs`, `select_pairs_at`), `signals.py` (`SpreadState`, `Event`, `PairState`, `step`, `run_pair`, `zscore`, `latest_zscore`, `zscore_lookback`, `spread`, `rolling_std`, `rolling_coint_pvalue`, `expected_return`, `confidence`, `PairSignal`, `to_signal`, `target_weights`, `PairsSnapshot`, `pairs_snapshot`), `backtest.py` (`pairs_backtest`, `PairsBacktestResult`, `TRADE_COLUMNS`, `SELECTION_COLUMNS`, `DAILY_COLUMNS`); Stage 17 `weights_backtest` (extracted from `vector_backtest`, behaviour unchanged); CLI `atlas research pairs --as-of [--universe] [--out]`; config `cfg.quant.statarb` (`StatArbConfig`; YAML `config/fund.yaml › quant.statarb`); tests `tests/unit/quant/statarb/` (126)
- Key interfaces: `select_pairs(log_prices, sectors, *, config, formed=None) -> list[Pair]` (last `formation` rows; same sector; caps); `step(state, z, *, broken, half_life, config) -> (PairState, Event | None)` — the one state machine (Stage 39's pod should call it, not re-implement it); `PairSignal{pair_id, long, short, hedge_ratio, z, exp_return, confidence}` (pydantic, frozen; `exp_return` annualised fraction of the pair's gross notional); `target_weights(signals, *, pair_gross) -> Series` (dollar-neutral per pair, ±pair_gross/2); `pairs_backtest(returns, sectors, *, start, end, members=None, config=None, progress=None)`; decision timing: z and selection at session t use closes through t−1, trade at t's close (Stage 17)
- Decisions: Decisions log rows dated 2026-11-09, stage 23 (execution, interpretations incl. the 504-session formation window, the false-positive test redesign, the Stage 17 refactor, mutation check and in-sample result)
- Collisions with the overall plan: none. Readings: correlation on 252 sessions (brief) but EG / β / half-life / Hurst on 504 (EG power); dollar-neutral (brief) rather than β-neutral legs; OLS rather than Kalman β
- In-sample results (real lake, 2017-01-03 .. 2021-12-31, nothing tuned): 564 names, 60 monthly formations, ~30 pairs selected per month (503 distinct; NWS~NWSA, AWK~WEC, FITB~TFC, AEP~XEL most frequent); **net Sharpe 0.10** (ann +0.21%, vol 2.10%, max DD 5.75%), **gross Sharpe 0.83**; costs 7.71% over 5 years (annual turnover 30.9, mean gross 0.49 of NAV); 1502 trades, hit rate 60.1%, mean trade +0.14% per unit pair gross (round trip 0.10%), mean held 8.4 sessions; exits: exit 1133, deselected 281, breakdown 66, stop 7, time stop 6; yearly net 2017 +0.07%, 2018 −0.64%, 2019 −0.66%, 2020 +2.68%, 2021 −0.49%
- Known gaps → backlog: the false-positive rate is the EG test's own size (~4%; multiple-testing control); thin edge after costs (cost-aware entry, keep open pairs through reselection, β-neutral legs — walk-forward in Stage 39 / 58); no borrow cost; static sectors; Johansen baskets and Kalman β (brief Out of scope)
- Next stage should know: a real-lake pairs backtest over 5 years takes ~25 min awake (60 formations; late-2020 onwards ~60 s each as more pairs pass the prefilter); the Mac sleeps on battery with the lid closed, which stretches background runs to hours — request keep-awake and keep it plugged in. `mutate.py` edits `src/` in place: never run it alongside tests or a real-lake run that is still importing; it restores the file on SIGTERM

### Stage 24 — LLM gateway, model tiers & cost budget — DONE 2026-11-10
- Folder: [stages/stage-24-llm-gateway/](stages/stage-24-llm-gateway/) (`plan.md`, `worklog.md`, `verification.md`, `artifacts/` incl. the live run, coverage and the mutation run)
- Built: `atlas.llm` — `gateway.py` (`LLMGateway`, `build_gateway`, `build_provider`), `schemas.py` (`Message`, `ToolSpec`, `ToolCall`, `Usage`, `LLMResult`, `ProviderRequest`, `ProviderResponse`, `LLMProvider`), `providers/openai_compat.py` (`OpenAICompatibleProvider`, DeepSeek by default), `providers/anthropic.py` (`AnthropicProvider`, optional), `providers/fake.py` (`FakeLLM`, `FakeReply`), `cost.py` (`rates_at`, `cost_usd`, `UsageRecord`, `CostLedger`), `budget.py` (`BudgetGuard`), `hygiene.py` (`data_block`, `DATA_POLICY`); native DuckDB table `llm_usage` + `Database.insert_llm_usage`; `BudgetExceeded`, `LLMTransientError`; config `llm.*` and the `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` secret; the `fake_llm` test fixture
- Key interfaces: `LLMGateway(provider, config, *, ledger=None, clock=None, sleep=..., timer=...)`; `gateway.complete(messages, *, tier="quick"|"deep", schema=None, tools=None, temperature=0.0, max_tokens=None, metadata=None) -> LLMResult` (`content`, `parsed`, `usage`, `cost_usd`, `model`, `latency_ms`, `cache_hit`, plus `tool_calls`, `finish_reason`, `attempts`); `build_gateway(config, *, lake=None)` (with a lake, usage goes to `llm_usage`); in tests `LLMGateway(fake_llm, AtlasConfig())` with `fake_llm.add(agent, step, *replies)`; `metadata={"agent": ..., "step": ...}` keys FakeLLM scripts and the ledger; wrap untrusted text with `data_block(text, source="news:123")`
- Decisions: Decisions log rows dated 2026-11-09 / 2026-11-10, stage 24 (D1–D10)
- Collisions with the overall plan: none
- Deviations from brief: structured output uses JSON schema only (forced tool use is rejected by both providers' current models); `hygiene.py` is an extra file; `LLMResult` has three extra fields (`tool_calls`, `finish_reason`, `attempts`); the Anthropic provider ignores `temperature`
- Known gaps → backlog: Claude refusal fallbacks; Haiku long-prompt price tier; reasoning replay in tool loops (Stage 26); cache-key notes (Stage 25); price snapshot and holidays; no streaming / async; provisional budgets
- Next stage should know: catch `LLMError` to fail safe (it covers `BudgetExceeded` and exhausted retries); `ConfigError` means a model without a price. Costs and the daily budget follow the **wall clock in UTC**, also inside a backtest. `LLMResult.model` is the configured tier id, not the provider's served name. The live test needs `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` exported (a worktree has no `.env`) and `api.deepseek.com` allowed; it costs about 0.0005 USD. A full `make check` now takes about 6.5 minutes (4037 tests). mutmut works on `src/atlas/llm` in about 40 s (copy the whole `tests/` folder in `also_copy`)
